# wiz3D "We See 3D"

A universal stereoscopic 3D wrapper for DirectX 7-11, OpenGL, AMD HD3D, and Nvidia 3D Vision. Based on iZ3D. 

**iZ3D** meant "**I** See 3D", so **wiz3D** means "**We** See 3D"

<img width="5760" height="1080" alt="vlcsnap-2026-04-05-21h46m56s809_Parralel _and_Cross" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/b8d2c574-2962-4bfa-b15d-380956552a32" />
<img width="5760" height="1080" alt="vlcsnap-2026-04-03-22h37m17s146_Parralel _and_Cross" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/89859e2f-915f-499f-a307-b8738d0e561f" />
<img width="5760" height="1080" alt="Screenshot 2026-03-26 190057_Parralel _and_Cross" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/493f2e32-1c3b-42c6-984d-ef9eacf96620" />

---

## What Is This?
wiz3D is an open-source stereoscopic 3D wrapper that hooks into DirectX, OpenGL, and AMD HD3D native and Nvidia 3D Vision games to generate real-time stereo 3D output (Half Side-by-Side, Anaglyph, Simulated Reality, etc.) without requiring kernel drivers or proprietary hardware or software.

iZ3D was a commercial product (~2002–2010) and one of the pioneers in modding games for stereoscopic 3D using kernel-level driver injection. The original developers kindly open-sourced the code under the MIT license, hosted by [bo3b/iZ3D](https://github.com/bo3b/iZ3D).

This project modernises that source code, replaces kernel-level hooks with a proxy DLL loader, and expands the scope to re-enable stereoscopic 3D in games that have native AMD HD3D and Nvidia 3D Vision support.

### Credits & Acknowledgements
wiz3D stands on the shoulders of some incredible people and projects. Huge thanks to:
- **Vadim and the original iZ3D development team**, for building the groundbreaking stereoscopic 3D wrapper that this project modernises, and for releasing it to the community.
- **[bo3b](https://github.com/bo3b)**, for hosting the iZ3D source archive at [bo3b/iZ3D](https://github.com/bo3b/iZ3D) under an MIT license, and for laying the groundwork for people to contribute.
- **[@AkshayUHegde](https://github.com/AkshayUHegde)**, for PR #31, which lands the full DX11 Stereo-Injector pipeline in 0.4.0 (COM-wrap, DXBC analyser, 14 per-game profiles).
- **[oneup03](https://github.com/oneup03)** for [NV3DLib](https://github.com/oneup03/NV3D-Lib), LGPLv2.1, which 0.5.0 statically links into every output-producing wrapper to drive NVIDIA 3D Vision shutter glasses on modern Windows (`OutputMode=3DVisionGlasses`). wiz3D authored and upstreamed NV3DLib's native DX9 backend as part of this work. See `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt` entry 11 for the full LGPLv2.1 notice and build-from-source instructions.
- **[bo3b](https://github.com/bo3b)** (again), for the open-source [Katanga](https://github.com/bo3b/Katanga) 3D viewer. 0.5.0's `OutputMode=Katanga` publishes to Katanga's shared-texture protocol from every wrapper; no Katanga source is linked, so the protocol stays an API contract — see `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt` entry 12.
- Every contributor who has filed issues, PRs or game-test results. The compatibility tables below exist because of you.

## Current Status

* **Nvidia 3D Vision 'Direct Mode':** ✅ **Mostly Working.** _(expanded in 0.4.0)_ DX9, DX10 and DX11 vendor proxies all ship. 0.4.0 adds the MinHook migration on d3d9, SR Weave port, `NativeProfile.xml` cross-session persistence, and the DX11 NVAPI stereo bridge (so games can drive wiz3D's separation/convergence from their own 3D settings).
* **AMD HD3D:** ✅ **Mostly Working!** HD3D games render stereo internally; the proxy enables rendering, captures the quad-buffer output and displays it using modern stereo-3D formats. Currently Half SBS / Half TAB work for about half the games; the rest still need output work. Deus Ex: Human Revolution + Director's Cut fixed in 0.4.0.
* **OpenGL Quad-Buffer Stereo:** ✅ **Partially Working.** Captures native OpenGL quad-buffer stereo output (`PFD_STEREO` / `GL_BACK_LEFT/RIGHT`) from games that produce it (id Tech 3/4, CAD, flight sims) and displays it in modern stereo formats. 0.4.0 migrates OpenGL to the unified OutputMode naming (SR Weave default where supported). Does not inject stereo into mono OpenGL games, that's a separate project planned for a later release.
* **DirectX 11:** ✅ **Mostly Working!** _(new in 0.4.0)_ Full DX11 COM-wrap pipeline, devices, deferred contexts, DXGI 1.2+ factories, DXBC analyser with per-shader view-projection capture, draw-time constant patching, HUD at scene depth, stereo scissors, deferred-light + decal + fog + screen-ray cameras, compute/UAV eye duplication, 3D Vision stereo texel for GameWorks ShadowLib. Per-game profiles ship for Battlefield 3, Battlefield Hardline, BioShock Infinite, Far Cry 3, Far Cry 4, Grand Theft Auto V (Legacy), Mass Effect: Andromeda, Mass Effect Legendary Edition, Max Payne 3, Metro Redux, NieR:Automata, Ryse: Son of Rome, Star Wars Jedi: Fallen Order and What Remains of Edith Finch.
* **DirectX 10:** ⚠️ **In Progress.** The DX10 vendor proxy was revived for 0.4.0 (SR stub + Win11 SDK fix) and builds again, but the DX10 analyser coverage is minimal, DX10 inherits DX11 scaffolding but no per-game profiles currently work. Testing DX10 titles is one of the biggest open contribution areas.
* **DirectX 9:** ✅ **Mostly Working!** `d3d9.dll` proxy loader works. Left 4 Dead 2 and many others run in full stereo 3D; profile system loads shader fixes and stereo settings for every originally-supported iZ3D game. Modern OSD + laser-sight crosshair ported from DX11 in 0.4.0.
* **DirectX 8:** ✅ **Partially Working.** Wrapper that converts DX8 to use the DX9 stereoisation path. Known-good on a handful of titles, needs broader coverage.
* **DirectX 7:** ⚠️ **In Progress.** Wrapper that converts DX7 to use the DX9 stereoisation path. DX7→DX9 conversion is working, present for testing.
* **Nvidia 3D Vision 'Automatic Mode':** ✅ **Partially Working.** Games that relied on NVIDIA's driver stereo injection, the ones listed have 3D Vision in-game settings, shader fixes and config options. wiz3D's stereo injector replaces the driver path, with the aim of letting those shader fixes and separation/convergence sliders apply to wiz3D's output. In 0.4.0 the NVAPI stereo bridge extends to DX11, so convergence and separation sliders now hook up via `NvAPI_Stereo_SetConvergence`/`SetSeparation` on both DX9 and DX11.

---

## Getting Started Playing

1. Launch the game before downloading, and set the resolution and refresh rate, and make sure fullscreen is enabled.
2. Download the latest release from the **Releases** tab.
3. Find the executable (`.exe`) of the game you want to play, and check its entry in the tables below, the **Install Path** column tells you which `wiz3D` subfolder to use.
4. Copy the **contents** of that subfolder (e.g. the contents of `Stereo-Injector/DX9/32-bit/` for a DX9 32-bit game) directly into the folder containing the game's `.exe`.
5. Launch the game. Stereo 3D should activate automatically! If it's an HD3D game, you may need to activate `Stereoscopic 3D` or `HD3D` in the in-game menu.

Configure your output mode (Half Side-by-Side, Anaglyph, SR Weave, etc.) and any other settings you'd like to modify using the included `wiz3D_Config.xml` file.

---

## wiz3D Game Test Results

*Legend:* ✅ ***Working** = Stereo output activated and playable.* ⚠️ ***Partial** = Stereo activated but with issues (crash, wrong settings, shader problems).* ❌ ***Not loading** = Wrapper not activating. **Untested** = I haven't tested yet or don't have access to that game*

The **Install Path** column shows which wiz3D folder you copy next to the game's `.exe`.

### Nvidia 3D Vision "Direct Mode" Games 
Games that render stereoscopic 3D themselves and display that via 3D Vision's 'Direct Mode'.

| Game | Install Path | Testing | Notes |
|------|--------------|---------|-------|
| Hard Reset | `3D-Vision-Direct/DX9/32-bit` | ✅ Working | Game must be set to 120Hz. |
| Hitman: Absolution | `3D-Vision-Direct/DX11/32-bit` | ✅ Working | Game supports 3D Vision Direct Mode and HD3D. |
| Oil Rush | `3D-Vision-Direct/DX11/32-bit` | ✅ Working | DX11 output only. |
| Tomb Raider (2013) | `3D-Vision-Direct/DX11/32-bit` | ✅ Mostly Working | Game supports 3D Vision Direct Mode and HD3D. Use the `EOSStub` subfolder if you need Epic Online Services disabled for SR Weave cohabitation. |

- **Excluded (Depth Map not loading, better support on HD3D):** *Battlefield 3*, *DiRT 2*, *DiRT 3*, *DiRT 3 Complete Edition*, *DiRT Showdown*, *DiRT Rally*, *F1 2010*, *F1 2011*, *F1 2012*, *F1 2013*, *GRID 2*, *GRID Autosport*.
- **Excluded (Works on HD3D and 3D Vision Direct Mode, HD3D recommended):** *World of Warcraft*. <sub>(Stereo3D removed in 2018 DX12 update)</sub>

### Nvidia 3D Vision "Automatic Mode" Games 
Games that used 3D Vision "Automatic Mode" driver stereo injection, but communicate with 3D Vision via in-game settings, built-in shader fixes, or config options. For our release, these use wiz3D's stereo injector instead, with the aim of making any shader fixes and separation/convergence sliders compatible with our injector. 0.4.0 wires the NVAPI stereo bridge on DX9 and DX11, so in-game sliders now move wiz3D's own stereo state.

## Keyboard Shortcuts

In-game hotkeys for adjusting stereo on the fly. All bindings are remappable in `wiz3D_Config.xml` under `<HotKeyList>`, see the header comment in that file for the key-code format.

| Key | Action |
| --- | ------ |
| `Numpad *` | Toggle stereo 3D on/off |
| `Numpad +` | Increase separation (stereo depth / eye distance) |
| `Numpad -` | Decrease separation (stereo depth / eye distance) |
| `Shift+Numpad +` | Increase convergence (push parallax plane further away) |
| `Shift+Numpad -` | Decrease convergence (pull parallax plane closer) |
| `Numpad /` | Toggle auto-focus (auto-adjusts convergence based on scene depth) |
| `Numpad 7` / `8` / `9` | Switch between three saved stereo presets |
| `Ctrl+F8` | Swap left/right eyes (for inverted setups) |
| `Shift+Numpad *` | Open the on-screen wizard overlay |
| `Shift+F1` | Toggle on-screen hotkey hints |
| `Ctrl+F1` | Toggle status strip + FPS counter |
| `Ctrl+Numpad /` | Toggle laser sight (crosshair anchored at screen depth) |
| `Numpad 0` | **ShaderHunter**: enter / exit hunt mode (DX9 + DX11) — see [Profile Building & Shader Fixes](#profile-building--shader-fixes) |
| `Numpad 1` / `Numpad 2` | Hunt-mode only: cycle to previous / next drawn shader |
| `Numpad 3` | Hunt-mode only: capture the currently hidden shader to `wiz3D_Shaders\<Game>\Hunter\` |
| `Ctrl+F5` | Reload `BaseProfile.xml` live (apply profile edits without restarting the game) |
| `PrintScreen` | Save a stereo screenshot to `My Pictures\wiz3D Screenshots\<Game>\` (default format `.png`; pick PNG/PNS/JPEG/JPS/BMP/DDS via `<ScreenshotType>` in `wiz3D_Config.xml`) |
| `Ctrl+PrintScreen` | Save a stereo screenshot without applying the display's gamma ramp |

---

## Profile Building & Shader Fixes

Most games just work with wiz3D's built-in per-engine profiles (`BaseProfile.xml` ships with coverage for UE3/UE4, CryEngine, RAGE, Dunia, Snowdrop, idTech 4/5, and 15+ others). When a game *mostly* works but a specific effect is wrong — HUD at the wrong depth, broken shadows, haloed decals, a reticle floating in space — you can fix just that effect without touching the rest of the game.

The fix lives in your copy of `BaseProfile.xml` next to the game `.exe`, so it's game-local, text-editable, and reversible.

### Quick workflow — DX11

1. **Launch the game.** Get to the scene with the broken effect.
2. **Press `Numpad 0`** to enter ShaderHunter mode. A `HUNT` strip appears at the bottom of the screen.
3. **Press `Numpad 1` / `Numpad 2`** to cycle through the shaders used this frame. Each shader is *hidden* (its draws are skipped) one at a time — when the broken effect disappears, you've found the shader responsible.
4. **Press `Numpad 3`** to capture that shader. A `Shader saved` toast appears bottom-right with the filename. The report lands at `wiz3D_Shaders\<Game>\Hunter\0x<CRC>_<stage>_<timestamp>.txt` and contains the shader disassembly, matrix-CB hints, and a suggested `<ShaderPatch>` / `<Mono>` skeleton you can paste into `BaseProfile.xml`.
5. **Press `Numpad 0`** again to leave hunt mode.
6. **Edit `BaseProfile.xml`** next to the game `.exe`. Drop the suggested skeleton inside the game's `<Profile>` block. The simplest fix is `<SkipStereo/>` — mono-force that shader so the broken effect stops shifting between eyes.
7. **Press `Ctrl+F5`** in-game to reload the profile. No restart needed.
8. **Iterate.** When happy, open a GitHub issue or PR with your profile addition so other players benefit.

### Fix types, cheapest first

**`<SkipStereo/>` — mono-force one shader.** The 90% case: a HUD element or 2D overlay that should sit at screen depth. One line inside a `<ShaderPatch CRC="0x...">` block.

```xml
<ShaderPatch CRC="0x1D9628E2" Stage="VS" Note="Minimap compass">
    <SkipStereo/>
</ShaderPatch>
```

**CB-math features — the big ones.** The engine's constant buffers already contain the view/projection matrices; wiz3D edits a few bytes before the right eye's draws so untouched game shaders naturally render per-eye. 37 declarative features cover shadow projection, deferred lights, screen-space rays, HUD depth, inverse-matrix cameras, UAV eye-duplication, etc. These are tagged in `BaseProfile.xml` under each `<Profile>` and documented inline in `S3DWrapper10/Feature*.cpp`.

**Inline ShaderOverride — hand-write HLSL.** For the shader-level cases a CB edit can't reach (custom depth outputs, loose scalar transforms, HUDs that bypass CBs). Paste HLSL inside the profile as a `CDATA` block; wiz3D compiles it at startup and swaps the game's bytecode. Sample the stereo-params SRV at `t125` (HelixMod / 3DMigoto convention) to apply your own per-eye offset:

```xml
<ShaderOverride CRC="0x7BCB56E1" Stage="VS"><![CDATA[
    // ...your HLSL...
    Texture2D<float4> stereo : register(t125);
    // ...sample at (0,0) for (separation, convergence, eye_sign, 0)...
]]></ShaderOverride>
```

### DX9

The DX9 wrapper has the same ShaderHunter + reload workflow (same hotkeys). Fix authoring uses the iZ3D-native declarative XML in `BaseProfile.xml`: `<Mono>` for shader-level stereo-off, `<Multiplier>` / `<ConvergenceShift>` for per-CRC stereo-param overrides, and `<Matrices>` for projection-matrix identification. See `docs/SHADER_FIXES.md` for the full technical breakdown.

### Mass-dump option

If you want every shader the game creates (not just the ones you hunt), flip `<DumpVS Value="1"/>` and `<DumpPS Value="1"/>` in `wiz3D_Config.xml`. Dumps land in `wiz3D_Shaders\<Game>\Dump\`. Useful when you have a known-good HelixMod fix from another wrapper and want to CRC-match the shaders yourself.

Developer shortcut: set the `WIZ3D_FORCE_DUMP=1` environment variable to auto-enable `DumpVS` + `DumpPS` across every game without having to flip the Config.xml knob per install — intended for local authoring workflows, never set in release/CI environments.

### Submitting fixes

Open a GitHub issue or PR with the game name, your `<Profile>` XML block, and a short note on what it fixes. Profiles merged into `BaseProfile.xml` ship for every player on the next release. By submitting, you agree to LGPL-2.1 for your profile content (the same licence the rest of the project uses).

---

## Building from Source

### Prerequisites

- **Visual Studio 2026** with the "Desktop development with C++" workload (v145 toolset)
- **Windows 11 SDK** (10.0.26100 or later)
- Git

### External SDKs (not included, download separately)

Several SDKs are excluded from the repository due to licensing or size. Download them and place them at the paths shown before building:

| SDK | Path in repo | Where to get it |
|-----|-------------|-----------------|
| Immersity LeiaSR SDK Win64 v1.36.2 + Win32 v1.34.10 | `lib/Simulated Reality/` | [https://support.immersity.ai/sdk/](https://support.immersity.ai/sdk/) and [Samsung Odyssey 3D Hub Installer](https://www.samsung.com/uk/support/model/LS27FG900XUXXU/#downloads) |
| NVAPI SDK | `lib/NVAPI/` and `lib/nvapi_2026/` | [https://github.com/NVIDIA/nvapi](https://github.com/NVIDIA/nvapi) |
| Boost 1.87 | `lib/boost/` | [https://www.boost.org/releases/1.87.0/](https://www.boost.org/releases/1.87.0/) |

`lib/PerfSDK/` is a deprecated NVIDIA proprietary SDK; all code referencing it is `#if 0`'d out, so you don't need it to build.

### Solutions

There are two solutions to build:

| Solution | What it builds |
|----------|---------------|
| `S3DDriver.sln` | All stereo wrapper DLLs, output method DLLs, NvApiProxy, vendor proxies |
| `wiz3D-proxy/wiz3D-proxy.sln` | Entry-point proxy loaders: `d3d8.dll`, `d3d9.dll`, `d3d10.dll`, `d3d11.dll`, `d3d12.dll`, `ddraw.dll`, `dxgi.dll`, `opengl32.dll`, `vulkan-1.dll` |

Build both in **Release \| Win32** for 32-bit games, or **Release \| x64** for 64-bit games.

#### One-shot build + deploy (recommended)

Run from the repo root in PowerShell:

```powershell
.\bin\build_and_deploy.ps1                # both archs, both solutions
.\bin\build_and_deploy.ps1 -Arch Win32    # x86 only
.\bin\build_and_deploy.ps1 -Arch x64      # x64 only
.\bin\build_and_deploy.ps1 -SkipBuild     # deploy only (after a manual VS build)
.\bin\build_and_deploy.ps1 -SkipProxy     # only build S3DDriver.sln
```

This builds both solutions and copies every freshly-built DLL into the right `releases/wiz3D/<api>/<arch>/` subfolder for you. If you'd rather drive MSBuild yourself (or use VS's F7), run `.\bin\deploy_to_releases.ps1` afterwards to populate the release tree.

### Testing a Build

1. Build both solutions (or run `.\bin\build_and_deploy.ps1`).
2. Copy the **contents** of the appropriate `releases/wiz3D/` subfolder (e.g. `Stereo-Injector/DX9/32-bit/`) directly into the game's `.exe` folder.
3. Set `wiz3D_Config.xml` or `HD3D_Config.xml` to match your display specifications
4. For some games (L4D2, Portal, HL2), copy into the `bin/` subfolder instead.
5. Launch the game. Stereo should activate automatically.
6. Toggle stereo on and off using `Numpad *`.

---

## Contributing

Contributions are very welcome. Key areas where help is most needed:

1. **Game testing**, test with DX7-11 and OpenGL games and add results to the tables above.
2. **DX10 completion**, the DX10 vendor proxy builds in 0.4.0 but has no per-game analyser coverage; porting the DX11 profiles across is a high-impact effort.
3. **DX7/8 stereo**, basic wrappers pass through to DX9 but stereo initialisation isn't working on every title yet.
4. **OpenGL stereo**, current OpenGL support is quad-buffer passthrough only; a mono-GL injector is on the roadmap.
5. **Output modes**, test the various stereo output plugins (SBS, anaglyph, interlaced, SR Weave, shutter).

Please open an issue before starting large changes. For game test results, edit the tables in `README.md` directly and open a PR.

---

## License & Commercial Use

The original legacy iZ3D code included in this repository remains under its original **MIT License**.

All new modifications, proxy DLLs, hooks, and modernisations introduced by the **wiz3D** project are licensed under the **GNU Lesser General Public License v2.1 (LGPLv2.1)** (see `LICENSE`).

**What this means for the community:** wiz3D is free for gamers, modders, and the open-source community to use, modify, and distribute. Thanks to the LGPLv2.1, this wrapper can be legally and safely injected into proprietary, closed-source games. However, any modifications made directly to the wiz3D wrapper codebase itself must remain open-source and be shared back with the community under the same license.

FILE: Common.props
================================================
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<Project DefaultTargets="Build" ToolsVersion="4.0" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
	<ItemDefinitionGroup>
		<ClCompile>
			<AdditionalIncludeDirectories>$(SolutionDir);$(SolutionDir)Shared\;%(AdditionalIncludeDirectories)</AdditionalIncludeDirectories>
			<PreprocessorDefinitions>_SILENCE_STDEXT_HASH_DEPRECATION_WARNINGS;STRSAFE_NO_DEPRECATE;%(PreprocessorDefinitions)</PreprocessorDefinitions>
			<WarningLevel>Level3</WarningLevel>
			<MultiProcessorCompilation>true</MultiProcessorCompilation>
			<DisableSpecificWarnings>4127;4201;4203;4342;4347;4514;4710;4820;4986;4996;%(DisableSpecificWarnings)</DisableSpecificWarnings>
			<TreatSpecificWarningsAsErrors>4289;4545;4546;4549;4714;4836;4905;4906;4946 </TreatSpecificWarningsAsErrors>
		</ClCompile>
		<ResourceCompile>
			<AdditionalIncludeDirectories>$(SolutionDir)Shared\;%(AdditionalIncludeDirectories)</AdditionalIncludeDirectories>
		</ResourceCompile>
		<Link>
			<AdditionalOptions>/ignore:4006 /ignore:4099 %(AdditionalOptions)</AdditionalOptions>
		</Link>
	</ItemDefinitionGroup>
	<!-- Win32 only: /LARGEADDRESSAWARE (x64 is large-address-aware implicitly). -->
	<ItemDefinitionGroup Condition="'$(Platform)'=='Win32'">
		<Link>
			<LargeAddressAware>true</LargeAddressAware>
		</Link>
	</ItemDefinitionGroup>
	<!-- x64 only: WIN64 define (was the sole real difference in the old Common_x64.props). -->
	<ItemDefinitionGroup Condition="'$(Platform)'=='x64'">
		<ClCompile>
			<PreprocessorDefinitions>WIN64;%(PreprocessorDefinitions)</PreprocessorDefinitions>
		</ClCompile>
	</ItemDefinitionGroup>
</Project>

FILE: Directory.Build.props
================================================
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<Project xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
	<!-- 
    Set the platform toolset early enough for MSBuild to find the build tools.
    The original Common.props sets this too late (after Microsoft.Cpp.Default.props).
    Directory.Build.props is auto-imported before any project evaluation.
  -->
	<PropertyGroup>
		<PlatformToolset>v145</PlatformToolset>
		<!-- Pinned so fxc and other SDK-bin tools (shader compiler especially)
         produce deterministic output across machines. Bump when the team
         agrees to move; do NOT use the "10.0" wildcard. -->
		<WindowsTargetPlatformVersion>10.0.26100.0</WindowsTargetPlatformVersion>
		<!-- ATL is used pervasively (CComPtr, etc.) — enable it for all projects -->
		<UseOfAtl>Static</UseOfAtl>
	</PropertyGroup>

	<!-- DirectXSDK DxErr.lib (and other legacy DX libs) references _vsnwprintf /
           _vsnprintf as external symbols. These used to live inline in the CRT
           headers but were externalized; the Windows SDK ships them in
           legacy_stdio_definitions.lib. Without this, anything linking DxErr
           (directly or via OutputLib.lib, which merges DxErr's .objs in)
           fails with "unresolved external symbol __vsnwprintf". -->
	<ItemDefinitionGroup>
		<Link>
			<AdditionalDependencies>legacy_stdio_definitions.lib;%(AdditionalDependencies)</AdditionalDependencies>
		</Link>
	</ItemDefinitionGroup>

	<!-- DevIL's IL/il.h defaults its API decls to __declspec(dllimport), which
           emits __imp__ symbols at the callsite. We link the *-windows-static
           triplet's DevIL.lib, which only provides the plain symbols, so the
           header must be told this is a static-lib build. -->
	<ItemDefinitionGroup>
		<ClCompile>
			<PreprocessorDefinitions>IL_STATIC_LIB;%(PreprocessorDefinitions)</PreprocessorDefinitions>
		</ClCompile>
	</ItemDefinitionGroup>

	<!--BOOST_ALL_NO_LIB disables Boost's #pragma comment(lib, ...) auto-link
        directives. Our code only uses header-only Boost (shared_ptr,
        lexical_cast, pool, bind, etc.) but a transitive include chain emits an
        auto-link for boost_thread's *release* variant, which triggers an
        LNK2038 RuntimeLibrary mismatch in Debug builds (Debug is /MTd, the
        release boost_thread is /MT). With auto-link off and no actual boost
        compiled-lib symbol use, nothing needs to be added explicitly.
	-->
	<ItemDefinitionGroup>
		<ClCompile>
			<PreprocessorDefinitions>BOOST_ALL_NO_LIB;%(PreprocessorDefinitions)</PreprocessorDefinitions>
		</ClCompile>
	</ItemDefinitionGroup>

	<!--Add all Third Party Libs to the build. These are stored in the ThirdPartyLibs
		directory as a common file for all projects to use. 
    -->
	<Import Project="$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)ThirdPartyLibs\ThirdPartyLibs.props" />

	<!-- LlamaXML shim wiring for every project that touches XML through the
     shim layer — directly (S3DWrapper9/10, DX9GenerateCodeFromDump, S3DAPI)
     or transitively (OutputLib + every OutputMethod via the XMLStreamer.h
     include chain). See lib/LlamaXML_shim.props for the details.

     S3DAPI was previously excluded because it used TinyXML 2.6.2's
     QueryBoolAttribute (absent in ticpp's bundled 2.5.3). Those calls are
     now replaced with a QueryIntAttribute-based helper, so S3DAPI links
     ticpp like everyone else — one ABI for TiXmlNode across all DLLs.
	 -->
	<Import Project="$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)lib\LlamaXML_shim.props" />
	
</Project>



================================================
FILE: FinalRelease.props
================================================
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<Project DefaultTargets="Build" ToolsVersion="4.0" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
  <ImportGroup Label="PropertySheets">
    <Import Project="Common.props" />
  </ImportGroup>
  <PropertyGroup>
    <LinkIncremental>false</LinkIncremental>
  </PropertyGroup>
  <ItemDefinitionGroup>
    <ClCompile>
      <Optimization>MaxSpeed</Optimization>
      <InlineFunctionExpansion>AnySuitable</InlineFunctionExpansion>
      <IntrinsicFunctions>true</IntrinsicFunctions>
      <FavorSizeOrSpeed>Speed</FavorSizeOrSpeed>
      <!-- FINAL_RELEASE strips DEBUG_MESSAGE / DEBUG_TRACE1/2/3 call-sites
           out of hot paths (Trace.h: ZLOG_ALLOW_TRACING is defined only when
           FINAL_RELEASE isn't, so those macros become `if (FALSE) __noop`).
           Removed in 36b91313 (0.4.0) under the assumption that keeping
           traces live was costless; call-site density in BaseStereoRenderer
           / BaseSwapChain hot paths actually corrupts DX9 stereo in a
           subset of titles (GTA IV, L4D, AvP, HTS — issue #38). Reinstated
           for 0.4.1. zlog itself still ships and writes to disk:
           InitializeLogging(), gInfo.LogFileDirectory and the gInfo dump-flag
           fields were decoupled from this define (0.4.1 changes to
           GlobalData.h / Logging.h / Logging.cpp / dllmain.cpp /
           S3DWrapper9/Globals.cpp) so shipped builds keep the log-file
           plumbing while losing the trace noise. -->
      <PreprocessorDefinitions>IZ3D_BUILD;FINAL_RELEASE;_HAS_EXCEPTIONS=0;BOOST_NO_STD_TYPEINFO;_SECURE_ATL=0;_SECURE_SCL=0;ATLENSURE(expr)=(expr);%(PreprocessorDefinitions)</PreprocessorDefinitions>
      <ExceptionHandling>false</ExceptionHandling>
      <StringPooling>true</StringPooling>
      <RuntimeLibrary>MultiThreaded</RuntimeLibrary>
      <BufferSecurityCheck>false</BufferSecurityCheck>
      <FunctionLevelLinking>true</FunctionLevelLinking>
      <FloatingPointModel>Fast</FloatingPointModel>
      <DebugInformationFormat>ProgramDatabase</DebugInformationFormat>
      <EnableEnhancedInstructionSet>StreamingSIMDExtensions</EnableEnhancedInstructionSet>
    </ClCompile>
    <Link>
      <GenerateDebugInformation>true</GenerateDebugInformation>
      <OptimizeReferences>true</OptimizeReferences>
      <EnableCOMDATFolding>true</EnableCOMDATFolding>
    </Link>
  </ItemDefinitionGroup>
</Project>

FILE: NV3DLib.props
================================================
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<!--
  NV3DLib linker helper. Import from any wiz3D DLL that calls into the
  NV3D::Interface* API (SwapChain11Proxy, AmdQbProxy, 3DV-Direct DX11,
  S3DWrapperOGL). The props resolve to NV3DLib-mt.lib (Release) or
  NV3DLib-mtd.lib (Debug) for the current platform, matching the project's
  MultiThreaded CRT choice from Release.props / Debug.props.

  All four lib artifacts live under ThirdPartyLibs/NV3D-Lib/lib/.
  To refresh: clone oneup03/NV3D-Lib locally, build NV3DLib.sln, copy the
  resulting NV3DLib-{md,mt}{d,}.lib files into
  ThirdPartyLibs/NV3D-Lib/lib/{x32,x64}/{Debug,Release}-MT/.
-->
<Project ToolsVersion="4.0" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
  <!-- Resolve relative to THIS props file (wiz3D repo root) so the imports work from
       any solution (S3DDriver.sln, NvDirectMode.sln, standalone proxies). -->
  <PropertyGroup>
    <NV3DLibRoot>$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)ThirdPartyLibs\NV3D-Lib</NV3DLibRoot>
    <NV3DNvapiDir>$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)ThirdPartyLibs\NV3D-Lib\external\nvapi</NV3DNvapiDir>
  </PropertyGroup>
  <!-- Platform-specific .lib paths. Debug configs pull in -mtd (static CRT debug); Release/Final Release pull in -mt. -->
  <PropertyGroup Condition="'$(Platform)'=='Win32' and '$(Configuration)'=='Debug'">
    <NV3DLibLib>$(NV3DLibRoot)\lib\x32\Debug-MT\NV3DLib-mtd.lib</NV3DLibLib>
    <NV3DLibNvapi>$(NV3DNvapiDir)\x86\nvapi.lib</NV3DLibNvapi>
  </PropertyGroup>
  <PropertyGroup Condition="'$(Platform)'=='Win32' and '$(Configuration)'!='Debug'">
    <NV3DLibLib>$(NV3DLibRoot)\lib\x32\Release-MT\NV3DLib-mt.lib</NV3DLibLib>
    <NV3DLibNvapi>$(NV3DNvapiDir)\x86\nvapi.lib</NV3DLibNvapi>
  </PropertyGroup>
  <PropertyGroup Condition="'$(Platform)'=='x64' and '$(Configuration)'=='Debug'">
    <NV3DLibLib>$(NV3DLibRoot)\lib\x64\Debug-MT\NV3DLib-mtd.lib</NV3DLibLib>
    <NV3DLibNvapi>$(NV3DNvapiDir)\amd64\nvapi64.lib</NV3DLibNvapi>
  </PropertyGroup>
  <PropertyGroup Condition="'$(Platform)'=='x64' and '$(Configuration)'!='Debug'">
    <NV3DLibLib>$(NV3DLibRoot)\lib\x64\Release-MT\NV3DLib-mt.lib</NV3DLibLib>
    <NV3DLibNvapi>$(NV3DNvapiDir)\amd64\nvapi64.lib</NV3DLibNvapi>
  </PropertyGroup>
  <ItemDefinitionGroup>
    <ClCompile>
      <AdditionalIncludeDirectories>$(NV3DLibRoot)\include;%(AdditionalIncludeDirectories)</AdditionalIncludeDirectories>
    </ClCompile>
    <Link>
      <!-- NV3DLib statically links its callers' NVAPI + D3D9 symbols; include the per-arch nvapi
           lib + d3d9.lib so the final DLL resolves them. -->
      <AdditionalDependencies>$(NV3DLibLib);$(NV3DLibNvapi);d3d9.lib;%(AdditionalDependencies)</AdditionalDependencies>
    </Link>
  </ItemDefinitionGroup>
</Project>

FILE: Release.props
================================================
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<Project DefaultTargets="Build" ToolsVersion="4.0" xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/developer/msbuild/2003">
  <ImportGroup Label="PropertySheets">
    <Import Project="Common.props" />
  </ImportGroup>
  <PropertyGroup>
    <LinkIncremental>false</LinkIncremental>
  </PropertyGroup>
  <ItemDefinitionGroup>
    <ClCompile>
      <Optimization>MaxSpeed</Optimization>
      <InlineFunctionExpansion>OnlyExplicitInline</InlineFunctionExpansion>
      <IntrinsicFunctions>true</IntrinsicFunctions>
      <FavorSizeOrSpeed>Speed</FavorSizeOrSpeed>
      <PreprocessorDefinitions>IZ3D_BUILD;_HAS_EXCEPTIONS=0;BOOST_NO_STD_TYPEINFO;_HAS_ITERATOR_DEBUGGING=0;%(PreprocessorDefinitions)</PreprocessorDefinitions>
      <ExceptionHandling>false</ExceptionHandling>
      <StringPooling>true</StringPooling>
      <RuntimeLibrary>MultiThreaded</RuntimeLibrary>
      <BufferSecurityCheck>false</BufferSecurityCheck>
      <FloatingPointModel>Fast</FloatingPointModel>
      <DebugInformationFormat>ProgramDatabase</DebugInformationFormat>
      <EnableEnhancedInstructionSet>StreamingSIMDExtensions2</EnableEnhancedInstructionSet>
    </ClCompile>
    <Link>
      <GenerateDebugInformation>true</GenerateDebugInformation>
      <OptimizeReferences>true</OptimizeReferences>
      <EnableCOMDATFolding>true</EnableCOMDATFolding>
    </Link>
  </ItemDefinitionGroup>
</Project>

8. OpenGL3DVision (Helifax)
============================================================================
OpenGL 3D Vision wrapper — Windows opengl32.dll proxy that adds NVIDIA 3D
Vision support to OpenGL games via shader injection and FBO-based per-eye
rendering.
https://github.com/Helifax/OpenGL3DVision

Used in:
  (a) NvDirectMode/d3d9, /d3d10, /d3d11 magic-header SBS capture path
      (NvDirectMode/{d3d9,d3d10,d3d11}/magic_header_capture.{h,cpp})
  (b) NvDirectMode/opengl32 (source only — not shipped in v0.2.x releases)

(a) Magic-header SBS capture: the NVSTEREOIMAGEHEADER struct + signature
constant (0x4433564E "NV3D") + flag bits (SIH_SWAP_EYES / SIH_SCALE_TO_FIT)
+ the documented surface convention (W*2 × H+1 stereo blit with the header
in the last row) come from OpenGL3DVision's src/src/opengl_3dv.cpp. WiZ3D
implements the *consumer* side of that protocol — detecting these surfaces
when games (or wrappers like OpenGL3DVision itself) submit them, and
splitting them per-eye for our own composite pipeline. This is the
documented NVIDIA 3D Vision SDK "Stereo Blit Defaults" technique; the
OpenGL3DVision source is the clearest publicly-available reference for it.

(b) NvDirectMode opengl32 (legacy): the previous OpenGL Direct Mode wrapper
adapted OpenGL3DVision's two-FBO per-eye design (one FBO per eye sharing a
depth/stencil renderbuffer) and its wglGetProcAddress-based hook strategy
for GL extension functions. As of v0.2.1 that opengl32 build is no longer
shipped — magic-header capture (a) on the DX9/10/11 side picks up
OpenGL3DVision-driven games via its OpenGL↔DX interop bridge automatically.
The opengl32 source is kept in the tree (NvDirectMode/opengl32/) but not
deployed.

Neither path redistributes or uses OpenGL3DVision's shader injection,
FPS injection, or 3D Vision profile management code.

Copyright (c) 2015 Octavian Mihai Vasilovici aka Helifax.

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights
to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell
copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is
furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:

The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in
all copies or substantial portions of the Software.

THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,
FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE AND NONINFRINGEMENT. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,
OUT OF OR IN CONNECTION WITH THE SOFTWARE OR THE USE OR OTHER DEALINGS IN
THE SOFTWARE.

11. NV3DLib
============================================================================
NV3DLib — NVIDIA 3D Vision shutter-glasses output library
https://github.com/oneup03/NV3D-Lib
Author: oneup03

Used in: S3DWrapperD3D9.dll, S3DWrapperD3D10.dll (DX10/11), atidxx32.dll,
atidxx64.dll (HD3D), S3DWrapperOGL.dll, NvDirectMode d3d9.dll /
d3d11.dll. Statically linked (NV3DLib-mt.lib / NV3DLib-mtd.lib). Drives
OutputMode=3DVisionGlasses on every wiz3D wrapper via NV3D-Lib's
InterfaceDX9 / InterfaceDX11 / InterfaceOGL backends.

Distributed under the GNU Lesser General Public License, version 2.1.
See ThirdPartyLibs/NV3D-Lib/LICENSE for the full text.

The static libraries used at link time (NV3DLib-{md,mt}{d,}.lib) are
vendored into this repository under ThirdPartyLibs/NV3D-Lib/, together
with the public header (include/NV3D.hpp) and the NVAPI import libs
NVIDIA ships with NV3D-Lib (external/nvapi/). The upstream source lives
at the URL above; wiz3D does not redistribute it in this tree, but the
build artifacts here are reproducible from the exact upstream commits
each wiz3D release links against, so users can relink wiz3D's binaries
against a modified version of NV3DLib per LGPL v2.1 clause 6.

LGPLv2.1 Section 6 compliance: a user wishing to replace NV3DLib with
a modified version may clone oneup03/NV3D-Lib, build NV3DLib.sln,
drop the resulting NV3DLib-{md,mt}{d,}.lib files into
ThirdPartyLibs/NV3D-Lib/lib/{x32,x64}/{Release,Debug}-{MD,MT}/,
and rebuild the relevant wiz3D DLLs from their vcxproj files.

The wiz3D project authored the NV3DLib native-DX9 backend
(src/backends/dx9_backend.{h,cpp} + the InterfaceDX9 API in
include/NV3D.hpp), contributed back upstream under the same LGPLv2.1.

Copyright (C) oneup03 and NV3DLib contributors.
Copyright (C) wiz3D contributors (DX9 backend contribution).

                  GNU LESSER GENERAL PUBLIC LICENSE
                       Version 2.1, February 1999

12. Katanga shared-texture protocol (API contract only, no linked code)
============================================================================
Katanga — open-source NVIDIA 3D Vision content viewer
https://github.com/bo3b/Katanga

Used in: S3DWrapperD3D9.dll, S3DWrapperD3D10.dll (DX10/11), atidxx32.dll,
atidxx64.dll (HD3D), S3DWrapperOGL.dll, NvDirectMode d3d9.dll /
d3d11.dll. OutputMode=Katanga publishes a wide-SBS DX11 KMT shared
texture under the "Local\KatangaMappedFile" + "KatangaSetupMutex"
protocol that Katanga opens and displays.

NO SOURCE OR BINARY IS LINKED. The Katanga producer helpers
(Shared/KatangaProducer.{h,cpp}, Shared/KatangaProducerDX9.{h,cpp},
S3DWrapperOGL/KatangaOGL.{h,cpp}) are wiz3D-authored implementations
of the public shared-memory protocol; no code from Katanga's GPL-3.0
tree is incorporated. The Katanga application itself is downloaded
separately by the end user from the URL above and remains under its
own GPL-3.0 licence.

protocol.

FILE: AmdAdlProxy/AmdAdlProxy.cpp
================================================
// AmdAdlProxy.cpp
// Fake AMD Display Library (ADL) proxy DLL.
// Builds as atiadlxy.dll (x86) / atiadlxx.dll (x64).
//
// Some HD3D games (Codemasters EGO engine: DiRT Showdown, GRID 2, F1 series)
// load atiadlxy.dll / atiadlxx.dll to check for AMD hardware and stereo
// capabilities BEFORE loading atidxx32.dll for quad-buffer stereo.  On NVIDIA
// systems the ADL DLLs don't exist, so the games give up on HD3D.
//
// This proxy returns fake AMD adapter info (VendorId 0x1002) so the games
// proceed to load atidxx32.dll / atidxx64.dll (our AmdQbProxy).

#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define NOMINMAX
#include <windows.h>
#include "..\Shared\version.h"
#include <cstring>

// ---- ADL constants and types ------------------------------------------------

#define ADL_MAX_PATH            256
#define ADL_OK                  0
#define ADL_ERR                 -1
#define ADL_ERR_NOT_INIT        -2
#define ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM   -3
#define ADL_ERR_NOT_SUPPORTED   -8

typedef void* (__stdcall* ADL_MAIN_MALLOC_CALLBACK)(int);

struct AdapterInfo
{
    int  iSize;
    int  iAdapterIndex;
    char strUDID[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    int  iBusNumber;
    int  iDeviceNumber;
    int  iFunctionNumber;
    int  iVendorID;
    char strAdapterName[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    char strDisplayName[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    int  iPresent;
    int  iExist;
    char strDriverPath[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    char strDriverPathExt[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    char strPNPString[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    int  iOSDisplayIndex;
};

struct ADLVersionsInfo
{
    char strDriverVer[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    char strCatalystVersion[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    char strCatalystWebLink[ADL_MAX_PATH];
};

struct ADLDisplayID
{
    int iDisplayLogicalIndex;
    int iDisplayPhysicalIndex;
    int iDisplayLogicalAdapterIndex;
    int iDisplayPhysicalAdapterIndex;
};

// ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_* flags
#define ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_DISPLAYCONNECTED      0x00000001
#define ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_DISPLAYMAPPED         0x00000002
#define ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_MANNER_SINGLE         0x00000010

struct ADLDisplayInfo
{
    ADLDisplayID displayID;
    int iDisplayControllerIndex;
    char strDisplayName[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    char strDisplayManufacturerName[ADL_MAX_PATH];
    int iDisplayType;          // ADL_DT_*
    int iDisplayOutputType;    // ADL_DOT_*
    int iDisplayConnector;     // ADL_DC_*
    int iDisplayInfoMask;
    int iDisplayInfoValue;
};

struct ADLMode
{
    int iAdapterIndex;
    ADLDisplayID displayID;
    int iXPos;
    int iYPos;
    int iXRes;
    int iYRes;
    int iColourDepth;
    float fRefreshRate;
    int iOrientation;
    int iModeFlag;
    int iModeMask;
    int iModeValue;
};

struct ADLDisplayMap
{
    int iDisplayMapIndex;
    ADLMode displayMode;
    int iNumDisplayTarget;
    int iFirstDisplayTargetArrayIndex;
    int iDisplayMapMask;
    int iDisplayMapValue;
};

struct ADLDisplayTarget
{
    ADLDisplayID displayID;
    int iDisplayMapIndex;
    int iDisplayTargetMask;
    int iDisplayTargetValue;
};

// ---- Globals

static HINSTANCE g_hSelf = nullptr;
static ADL_MAIN_MALLOC_CALLBACK g_mallocCb = nullptr;
static bool g_bInitialized = false;

// ---- Diagnostic log ---------------------------------------------------------

static void WriteLog(const char* msg)
{
    wchar_t dir[MAX_PATH] = {};
    if (g_hSelf)
    {
        GetModuleFileNameW(g_hSelf, dir, MAX_PATH);
        wchar_t* p = wcsrchr(dir, L'\\');
        if (p) p[1] = L'\0';
    }
    if (!dir[0] && !GetTempPathW(MAX_PATH, dir)) return;

    wchar_t path[MAX_PATH];
    wcscpy_s(path, dir);
    wcscat_s(path, L"wiz3D_Logs");
    CreateDirectoryW(path, NULL);
    wcscat_s(path, L"\\HD3D_adl.log");
    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(path, FILE_APPEND_DATA,
                           FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
                           nullptr, OPEN_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    DWORD written;
    WriteFile(h, msg, static_cast<DWORD>(strlen(msg)), &written, nullptr);
    CloseHandle(h);
}

// ---- ADL function implementations ------------------------------------------

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Main_Control_Create(ADL_MAIN_MALLOC_CALLBACK callback, int iEnumConnectedAdapters)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Main_Control_Create\n");
    g_mallocCb = callback;
    g_bInitialized = true;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Main_Control_Destroy()
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Main_Control_Destroy\n");
    g_bInitialized = false;
    g_mallocCb = nullptr;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Main_Control_Refresh()
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Main_Control_Refresh\n");
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Main_Control_IsFunctionValid(HMODULE hModule, const char* lpProcName)
{
    char buf[300];
    wsprintfA(buf, "[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Main_Control_IsFunctionValid(%s)\n",
              lpProcName ? lpProcName : "(null)");
    WriteLog(buf);
    if (lpProcName && GetProcAddress(g_hSelf, lpProcName))
        return 1;
    return 0;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Adapter_NumberOfAdapters_Get(int* lpNumAdapters)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Adapter_NumberOfAdapters_Get -> 1\n");
    if (!lpNumAdapters) return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;
    *lpNumAdapters = 1;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Adapter_AdapterInfo_Get(AdapterInfo* lpInfo, int iInputSize)
{
    char buf[80];
    wsprintfA(buf, "[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Adapter_AdapterInfo_Get(size=%d)\n", iInputSize);
    WriteLog(buf);
    if (!lpInfo || iInputSize < static_cast<int>(sizeof(AdapterInfo)))
        return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;

    memset(lpInfo, 0, sizeof(AdapterInfo));
    lpInfo->iSize            = sizeof(AdapterInfo);
    lpInfo->iAdapterIndex    = 0;
    strcpy_s(lpInfo->strUDID, "PCI_VEN_1002&DEV_67DF&SUBSYS_0000&REV_00");
    lpInfo->iBusNumber       = 1;
    lpInfo->iDeviceNumber    = 0;
    lpInfo->iFunctionNumber  = 0;
    lpInfo->iVendorID        = 0x1002;
    strcpy_s(lpInfo->strAdapterName,  "AMD Radeon RX 580 Series");
    strcpy_s(lpInfo->strDisplayName,  "\\\\.\\DISPLAY1");
    lpInfo->iPresent         = 1;
    lpInfo->iExist           = 1;
    strcpy_s(lpInfo->strDriverPath,    "\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Video\\{FAKE-AMD}\\0000");
    strcpy_s(lpInfo->strDriverPathExt, "\\Registry\\Machine\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Video\\{FAKE-AMD}\\0000");
    strcpy_s(lpInfo->strPNPString,     "PCI\\VEN_1002&DEV_67DF&SUBSYS_0000&REV_00\\0000");
    lpInfo->iOSDisplayIndex  = 0;

    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Adapter_Active_Get(int iAdapterIndex, int* lpStatus)
{
    char buf[80];
    wsprintfA(buf, "[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Adapter_Active_Get(adapter=%d)\n", iAdapterIndex);
    WriteLog(buf);
    if (!lpStatus) return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;
    *lpStatus = 1; // ADL_TRUE
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Adapter_ID_Get(int iAdapterIndex, int* lpAdapterID)
{
    char buf[80];
    wsprintfA(buf, "[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Adapter_ID_Get(adapter=%d)\n", iAdapterIndex);
    WriteLog(buf);
    if (!lpAdapterID) return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;
    *lpAdapterID = 0x67DF; // RX 580 device ID
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Adapter_Aspects_Get(int iAdapterIndex, char* lpAspects, int iSize)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Adapter_Aspects_Get\n");
    if (lpAspects && iSize > 0)
        strcpy_s(lpAspects, iSize, "Power;3D;Stereo;Display;Core");
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Adapter_Primary_Get(int* lpPrimaryAdapterIndex)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Adapter_Primary_Get\n");
    if (!lpPrimaryAdapterIndex) return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;
    *lpPrimaryAdapterIndex = 0;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Display_NumberOfDisplays_Get(int iAdapterIndex, int* lpNumDisplays)
{
    char buf[80];
    wsprintfA(buf, "[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Display_NumberOfDisplays_Get(adapter=%d)\n", iAdapterIndex);
    WriteLog(buf);
    if (!lpNumDisplays) return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;
    *lpNumDisplays = 1;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Display_DisplayInfo_Get(int iAdapterIndex, int* lpNumDisplays, void** lppInfo, int iForceDetect)
{
    char buf[80];
    wsprintfA(buf, "[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Display_DisplayInfo_Get(adapter=%d)\n", iAdapterIndex);
    WriteLog(buf);
    if (!lpNumDisplays || !lppInfo) return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;

    if (!g_mallocCb) { *lpNumDisplays = 0; *lppInfo = nullptr; return ADL_OK; }

    *lpNumDisplays = 1;
    ADLDisplayInfo* pInfo = static_cast<ADLDisplayInfo*>(g_mallocCb(sizeof(ADLDisplayInfo)));
    if (!pInfo) { *lpNumDisplays = 0; *lppInfo = nullptr; return ADL_ERR; }
    memset(pInfo, 0, sizeof(ADLDisplayInfo));

    pInfo->displayID.iDisplayLogicalIndex          = 0;
    pInfo->displayID.iDisplayPhysicalIndex         = 0;
    pInfo->displayID.iDisplayLogicalAdapterIndex    = 0;
    pInfo->displayID.iDisplayPhysicalAdapterIndex   = 0;
    pInfo->iDisplayControllerIndex = 0;
    strcpy_s(pInfo->strDisplayName, "\\\\.\\DISPLAY1");
    strcpy_s(pInfo->strDisplayManufacturerName, "Generic Monitor");
    pInfo->iDisplayType       = 3;   // ADL_DT_DIGITAL_FLAT_PANEL
    pInfo->iDisplayOutputType = 4;   // ADL_DOT_HDMI (common for stereo)
    pInfo->iDisplayConnector  = 12;  // ADL_DC_HDMI
    pInfo->iDisplayInfoMask   = ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_DISPLAYCONNECTED |
                                ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_DISPLAYMAPPED |
                                ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_MANNER_SINGLE;
    pInfo->iDisplayInfoValue  = ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_DISPLAYCONNECTED |
                                ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_DISPLAYMAPPED |
                                ADL_DISPLAY_DISPLAYINFO_MANNER_SINGLE;

    *lppInfo = pInfo;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Display_SLSMapIndex_Get(int iAdapterIndex, int iNumDisplayTarget,
    void* lpDisplayTarget, int* lpSLSMapIndex)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Display_SLSMapIndex_Get\n");
    if (lpSLSMapIndex) *lpSLSMapIndex = -1;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Display_SLSMapConfig_Get(int iAdapterIndex, int iSLSMapIndex,
    void* lpSLSMap, int* lpNumSLSTarget, void** lppSLSTarget,
    int* lpNumNativeMode, void** lppNativeMode,
    int* lpNumBezelMode, void** lppBezelMode,
    int* lpNumTransientMode, void** lppTransientMode,
    int* lpNumSLSOffset, void** lppSLSOffset, int iOption)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Display_SLSMapConfig_Get\n");
    if (lpNumSLSTarget) *lpNumSLSTarget = 0;
    if (lppSLSTarget) *lppSLSTarget = nullptr;
    if (lpNumNativeMode) *lpNumNativeMode = 0;
    if (lppNativeMode) *lppNativeMode = nullptr;
    if (lpNumBezelMode) *lpNumBezelMode = 0;
    if (lppBezelMode) *lppBezelMode = nullptr;
    if (lpNumTransientMode) *lpNumTransientMode = 0;
    if (lppTransientMode) *lppTransientMode = nullptr;
    if (lpNumSLSOffset) *lpNumSLSOffset = 0;
    if (lppSLSOffset) *lppSLSOffset = nullptr;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Display_Modes_Get(int iAdapterIndex, int iDisplayIndex,
    int* lpNumModes, void** lppModes)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Display_Modes_Get\n");
    if (lpNumModes) *lpNumModes = 0;
    if (lppModes) *lppModes = nullptr;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Display_DisplayMapConfig_Get(int iAdapterIndex,
    int* lpNumDisplayMap, void** lppDisplayMap,
    int* lpNumDisplayTarget, void** lppDisplayTarget, int iOption)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Display_DisplayMapConfig_Get\n");
    if (!lpNumDisplayMap || !lppDisplayMap || !lpNumDisplayTarget || !lppDisplayTarget)
        return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;

    if (!g_mallocCb)
    {
        *lpNumDisplayMap = 0; *lppDisplayMap = nullptr;
        *lpNumDisplayTarget = 0; *lppDisplayTarget = nullptr;
        return ADL_OK;
    }

    // Query current primary display resolution for realistic mode data
    int screenW = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSCREEN);
    int screenH = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSCREEN);
    if (screenW <= 0) screenW = 1920;
    if (screenH <= 0) screenH = 1080;

    // Allocate one display map
    ADLDisplayMap* pMap = static_cast<ADLDisplayMap*>(g_mallocCb(sizeof(ADLDisplayMap)));
    if (!pMap)
    {
        *lpNumDisplayMap = 0; *lppDisplayMap = nullptr;
        *lpNumDisplayTarget = 0; *lppDisplayTarget = nullptr;
        return ADL_ERR;
    }
    memset(pMap, 0, sizeof(ADLDisplayMap));
    pMap->iDisplayMapIndex                            = 0;
    pMap->displayMode.iAdapterIndex                   = 0;
    pMap->displayMode.displayID.iDisplayLogicalIndex   = 0;
    pMap->displayMode.displayID.iDisplayPhysicalIndex  = 0;
    pMap->displayMode.displayID.iDisplayLogicalAdapterIndex  = 0;
    pMap->displayMode.displayID.iDisplayPhysicalAdapterIndex = 0;
    pMap->displayMode.iXPos        = 0;
    pMap->displayMode.iYPos        = 0;
    pMap->displayMode.iXRes        = screenW;
    pMap->displayMode.iYRes        = screenH;
    pMap->displayMode.iColourDepth = 32;
    pMap->displayMode.fRefreshRate = 60.0f;
    pMap->displayMode.iOrientation = 0;
    pMap->iNumDisplayTarget                    = 1;
    pMap->iFirstDisplayTargetArrayIndex        = 0;
    pMap->iDisplayMapMask                      = 0x0003; // connected + mapped
    pMap->iDisplayMapValue                     = 0x0003;

    // Allocate one display target
    ADLDisplayTarget* pTgt = static_cast<ADLDisplayTarget*>(g_mallocCb(sizeof(ADLDisplayTarget)));
    if (!pTgt)
    {
        *lpNumDisplayMap = 0; *lppDisplayMap = nullptr;
        *lpNumDisplayTarget = 0; *lppDisplayTarget = nullptr;
        return ADL_ERR;
    }
    memset(pTgt, 0, sizeof(ADLDisplayTarget));
    pTgt->displayID.iDisplayLogicalIndex          = 0;
    pTgt->displayID.iDisplayPhysicalIndex         = 0;
    pTgt->displayID.iDisplayLogicalAdapterIndex    = 0;
    pTgt->displayID.iDisplayPhysicalAdapterIndex   = 0;
    pTgt->iDisplayMapIndex     = 0;
    pTgt->iDisplayTargetMask   = 0x0001; // connected
    pTgt->iDisplayTargetValue  = 0x0001;

    *lpNumDisplayMap    = 1;
    *lppDisplayMap      = pMap;
    *lpNumDisplayTarget = 1;
    *lppDisplayTarget   = pTgt;

    char logbuf[160];
    wsprintfA(logbuf, "[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Display_DisplayMapConfig_Get -> 1 map (%dx%d), 1 target\n",
              screenW, screenH);
    WriteLog(logbuf);
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Graphics_Versions_Get(ADLVersionsInfo* lpVersionsInfo)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Graphics_Versions_Get\n");
    if (!lpVersionsInfo) return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;
    memset(lpVersionsInfo, 0, sizeof(ADLVersionsInfo));
    strcpy_s(lpVersionsInfo->strDriverVer, "23.20.16041.5");
    strcpy_s(lpVersionsInfo->strCatalystVersion, "15.201.1151");
    strcpy_s(lpVersionsInfo->strCatalystWebLink, "http://support.amd.com");
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL_Display_EdidData_Get(int iAdapterIndex, int iDisplayIndex, void* lpEDIDData)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL_Display_EdidData_Get\n");
    return ADL_ERR_NOT_SUPPORTED;
}

// ---- ADL2 context-based API (used by newer games) ---------------------------

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL2_Main_Control_Create(ADL_MAIN_MALLOC_CALLBACK callback, int iEnumConnectedAdapters, void** context)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL2_Main_Control_Create\n");
    g_mallocCb = callback;
    g_bInitialized = true;
    if (context) *context = reinterpret_cast<void*>(1); // fake context
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL2_Main_Control_Destroy(void* context)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL2_Main_Control_Destroy\n");
    g_bInitialized = false;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL2_Adapter_NumberOfAdapters_Get(void* context, int* lpNumAdapters)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL2_Adapter_NumberOfAdapters_Get\n");
    if (!lpNumAdapters) return ADL_ERR_INVALID_PARAM;
    *lpNumAdapters = 1;
    return ADL_OK;
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL2_Adapter_AdapterInfo_Get(void* context, AdapterInfo* lpInfo, int iInputSize)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL2_Adapter_AdapterInfo_Get\n");
    return ADL_Adapter_AdapterInfo_Get(lpInfo, iInputSize);
}

extern "C" int __cdecl ADL2_Adapter_Active_Get(void* context, int iAdapterIndex, int* lpStatus)
{
    WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] ADL2_Adapter_Active_Get\n");
    return ADL_Adapter_Active_Get(iAdapterIndex, lpStatus);
}

// ---- DllMain ----------------------------------------------------------------

BOOL WINAPI DllMain(HINSTANCE hInst, DWORD reason, LPVOID)
{
    if (reason == DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH)
    {
        g_hSelf = hInst;
        DisableThreadLibraryCalls(hInst);
        WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH (wiz3D " DISPLAYED_VERSION ")\n");
    }
    else if (reason == DLL_PROCESS_DETACH)
    {
        WriteLog("[AmdAdlProxy] DLL_PROCESS_DETACH\n");
    }
    return TRUE;
}

// wiz3D AmdAdlProxy VERSIONINFO resource.
//
// Numeric version + git SHA come from Shared/version.h, which sources from
// the auto-generated bin/temp_version.h (regenerated each build by the
// Preparing project's pre-build step from the repo-root VERSION.txt).

#include <windows.h>
#include "..\Shared\version.h"

VS_VERSION_INFO VERSIONINFO
 FILEVERSION    VERSION_NUMBER
 PRODUCTVERSION PRODUCT_VERSION_NUMBER
 FILEFLAGSMASK  0x17L
#ifdef _DEBUG
 FILEFLAGS      0x1L
#else
 FILEFLAGS      0x0L
#endif
 FILEOS         0x4L
 FILETYPE       0x2L
 FILESUBTYPE    0x0L
BEGIN
    BLOCK "StringFileInfo"
    BEGIN
        BLOCK "000904b0"
        BEGIN
            VALUE "CompanyName",      VERSION_COMPANY
            VALUE "FileDescription",  "wiz3D AMD Display Library proxy"
            VALUE "FileVersion",      VERSION_STRING
            VALUE "InternalName",     "atiadlxy"
            VALUE "LegalCopyright",   VERSION_COPYRIGHT
            VALUE "OriginalFilename", "atiadlxy.dll"
            VALUE "ProductName",      FULL_PRODUCT_NAME
            VALUE "ProductVersion",   PRODUCT_VERSION_STRING
            VALUE "Build Date",       VERSION_BUILD_DATE_TIME
        END
    END
    BLOCK "VarFileInfo"
    BEGIN
        VALUE "Translation", 0x9, 1200
    END
END
